# Build 0642

CODAP Build 0642
was completed on Friday, August 26, 2022,
and released
that day.
It can be found here:
https://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0642
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since
Build 0641:

features...
* (No CODAP PT Story) Incorporate CFM changes to permit Bluetooth in sensor plugin.
* (No CODAP PT Story) Allow plugins to access Bluetooth.

bugs...
* [182669945](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/182669945) Data tips aren't showing in graph
* [183006130](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/183006130) Sampler should omit it's own dataset in Collector list
* [183006147](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/183006147) Sampler plugin should correctly handle attribute renames in translated versions
* [183061657](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/183061657) Sampler broken in Collector mode.

* [182701605](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/182701605) In Story Builder graphs don't always get their names saved in moments.

# Build 0559
CODAP Build 0559 was completed on Friday, October 16, 2020, and released on 
that day.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0559.
It was an emergency build, responding to Google Drive changes that broke our 
ability to open documents stored there. The single change was to reference the
Cloud File Manager, version 1.1.1.

CODAP Build 0523 was completed on Friday, March 20, 2020 and released that day.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0523.
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since
Build 0520.

features...
* [157211154](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157211154) When more than one case is selected navigation is to the first (or last) selected case rather than to the first case in the collection
* [158700917](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158700917) On touch it's very difficult to resize components or even to know that they can be resized. 
* [158703439](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158703439) An attribute name and description shows when the attribute is touch-clicked in the case table
* [166986432](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/166986432) Plugin that tracks user actions. User can click on an action and take the CODAP document back to the state it was in at the time of that action.
* [166986509](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/166986509) A plugin can obtain from CODAP a complete JSON representation of the current state of the document.
* [169743285](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169743285) On making a new attribute in case card user can immediately edit attribute name.
* [169764675](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169764675) CODAP should respond to document/get through plugin API

bugs...
* [121247039](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/121247039) iPad: Table scrolling does not work
* [150930667](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/150930667) On iPad when you create a new attribute the name field does not get edit focus. It should.
* [157178963](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157178963) Renaming collection in table doesn't carry over to card
* [158201775](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158201775) Table and case card have different behaviors
* [159419359](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/159419359) On iPad, the navigation of arrows in a case card looks disabled after being tapped
* [166423891](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/166423891) Case card tool palette doesn't automatically come up on first open
* [171282011](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171282011) Numeric values not plotting correctly
* [171288246](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171288246) Tiles positioned to the right of the screen reopens in the same area of the page even though it is not in display view
* [171388470](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171388470) Binned plot doesn't survive attempt to turn it into a scatterplot
* [171474665](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171474665) Plus icon on case table for making new attributes doesn't work on touch
* [171475756](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171475756) Attribute menu cannot be brought up by touching on column header in case table.
* [171587490](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171587490) None of the affordances on the case card respond to touch.
* [171709682](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171709682) Can't edit a data cell after attribute was moved to a new collection in case card mode
* [171752173](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171752173) Closing graph with histogram brings up crash alert
* [171793101](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171793101) Incorrect log message when switching from case table to case card

# Build 0520

CODAP Build 0520 was completed on Wednesday, March 4, 2020 and released on 
March 6th.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0520.
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since
Build 0517.

features...
* [171499763](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171499763) Hover over map point shows a data tip that includes the value of the collection's first attribute

bugs...
* [169191092](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169191092) Percentages do not update for split plots when you change row to column
* [169764744](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169764744) When user creates a component in CODAP, the document state sent to wds plugin has a zero width and height for the component.
* [170738038](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170738038) Component Dismiss Button and Minimize Button should have pointer cursor and tool tip
* [171269803](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171269803) Label format should be disabled when dot plot has been fused into bars
* [171269861](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171269861) Count remains in the y-axis after user unchecks Group into Bins in dot plots
* [171286951](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171286951) Moving an attribute from one axis to the other while in bin view crashes CODAP
* [171391514](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171391514) Google Drive downloads failing
* [171429312](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171429312) Certain attributes are not recognized as categorical for the purpose of splitting a plot.
* [171496453](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171496453) Plotting an attribute whose cases have no values yields a date time axis instead of default numeric axis.
* [171522617](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171522617) Attempting to drag attribute in presence of binned plot crashes CODAP
* [171549593](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171549593) CODAP freezes when you attempt to drag a movable value
* [171552408](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171552408) Dragging point in case plot, vertical movement is reversed

# Build 0517

CODAP Build 0517 was completed on Thursday, February 20, 2020 and released on 
February 21.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0517.
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since
Build 0513.

features...
* [131475149](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/131475149) CODAP should display Histograms
* [169881796](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169881796) Add ability to auto-resize case table to plugin api
* [169941550](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169941550) The Inspector pane for dot plots has a configuration menu icon.
* [169955157](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169955157) Configuration inspector pane has interface for grouping into bins
* [170393090](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170393090) Points in a binned dot plot get grouped into 5 bins by default
* [170837715](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170837715) Adapt eepsmedia noaa-cdo plugin
* [170837727](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170837727) Add selection of weather station from CODAP map.
* [170936842](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170936842) NOAA-weather plugin should allow arbitrary DataTypes
* [170959448](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170959448) NOAA-weather plugin: add lat/long and elev to weather dataset.
* [170965539](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170965539) Binned dot plot can be converted histogram
* [170965556](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170965556) Bin boundaries can be dragged to change bin width
* [171062898](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171062898) NOAA weather: Busy cursor and button transition when fetching data
* [171094637](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171094637) Record whether daily or monthly summary in parent table
* [171119605](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171119605) NOAA Weather app: selecting Weather Station in Weather data selects in Weather Station table.
* [171242753](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171242753) Microdata portal configuration
* [171269596](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171269596) Add a hover tip for binned plot labels

bugs...
* [169955800](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169955800) Tiles positioned to the right of the screen and closed causes inbound rule to break CODAP
* [170186981](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170186981) Sagemodeler csv import shows data blob instead of source
* [170286456](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170286456) SageModeler canvas does not resize as a CODAP component is outside browser window.
* [170286609](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170286609) SageModeler/CODAP does not see mouseup when mouse leaves canvas build 512
* [170524148](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170524148) Unable to add 2nd attribute to left y-axis
* [170605727](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170605727) Clicking on closed group in case table brings up index cell menu
* [170738301](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170738301) Case table cursor issues: add button, ghost line, index cells
* [170738416](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170738416) The expand/collapse icons between case tables should have a \"pointer\" cursor and tool tip.
* [170873584](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170873584) CSV Data import gets confused if units (or at least parens) are included in header row.
* [170873920](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170873920) Can't create graph with multiple y-axes if more than one dataset is present.
* [170936612](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170936612) NOAA-weather plugin should save and restore state
* [171062713](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171062713) \"di=\" brings plugin up in middle of screen.
* [171062828](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171062828) NOAA weather: Plugin should bring up bigger map with smaller points
* [171062935](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171062935) NOAA weather: Type error on data fetch
* [171114609](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171114609) Changing dot chart split layout to percent doesn't propagate to graphs other than the root.
* [171159639](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171159639) Can't plot right side axis when attribute formula evaluates 0/0 or NaN
* [171237686](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171237686) Count for histogram shows zero
* [171242508](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171242508) Import of CSV will fail in old documents if table matches existing
* [171248130](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171248130) Binned plot and histogram not sorting properly by legend categories.
* [171258581](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171258581) Abs function incorrectly reports the absolute value of a missing value as zero.
* [171260789](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171260789) For binned plot and histogram, dragging attribute to opposite axis doesn't work.
* [171267540](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171267540) On binned plot option to fuse dots into rectangles should not be present if the y-axis has an attribute.
* [171269890](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171269890) Count comes up as 0 in dot plots
* [171284758](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171284758) Selecting point after converting dot plot to binned dot plot crashes CODAP
* [171286198](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171286198) Bin width of zero crashes the webpage
* [171293128](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171293128) Attempting to split a histogram by a categorical attribute on the opposite axis messes up.
* [171293654](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171293654) Selecting a point in binned plot causes bin width and alignment to revert to default.
* [171293692](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171293692) Selecting a case should not wipe out the undo history
* [171368261](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/171368261) CODAP gets stuck when dragging bin boundary

http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0513.
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since
Build 0510.

features...

* [170063162](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170063162) Switch to displaying filename instead of data-uri when importing CSVs from SageModeler
* [170605546](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170605546) Easier attribute name editing in case table

bugs...

* [169266259](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/169266259) Loading of SageModeler docs results in unnecessary multiple reloadings of SageModeler.
* [170054005](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170054005) Removal of first y-axis attribute when there is more than one doesn't clear points for removed attribute.
* [170163784](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170163784) Import of random sample from large geojson data set is not working
* [170194391](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170194391) Set aside in the table doesn't show the correct mean/median value in the graph
* [170317222](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170317222) Should be able to dismiss case table attribute menu more easily
* [170372076](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170372076) Components have different Z order when restored
* [170442897](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170442897) Categories get repeated on 2nd categorical axis
* [170591296](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170591296) Creating new attribute in Case Table should commit last create
* [170604983](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170604983) Plugin name inference should ignore query parameters
* [170637108](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170637108) Data tip when there are two left y-axis only shows the second attribute data even if point hovered over is the first attribute data
* [170677844](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/170677844) Data tips disappear when 2nd left axis attribute is removed

# Build 0462

CODAP Build 0462 was completed on Thursday, October 4, 2018 and released on 
October 10.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0462.
The release includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since 
Build 0460.

features...
* [140188929](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140188929) TP-Sampler: With a spinner the percent that each wedge occupies shows on mouse hover
* [150486431](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/150486431) Have a toggle to \"freeze\" values without removing the formula of an attribute
* [151536172](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/151536172) In embedded mode, prevent Components from being dragged offscreen
* [152288555](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152288555) TP-Sampler: hidden spinner should be completely covered by a circle
* [153373624](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/153373624) Plugin should be able to cause any component to be selected
* [158802477](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158802477) Maps can display lat/long points from more than one dataset
* [158802692](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158802692) Maps can display multiple sets of polygons
* [158805098](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158805098) Maps have a \"layers\" inspector palette that provides control over which of the available base/point/polygon data are displayed.
* [158806846](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/158806846) Maps: More than one set of points or polygons can be plotted from a single dataset provided they are at different levels.
* [159643921](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/159643921) Maps: When a legend attribute is dropped on a map, the map applies it to the appropriate layer
* [159643936](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/159643936) Maps can display a legend for each of the legend attributes applied to the map
* [159711680](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/159711680) Map layers can be hidden or shown using check boxes in Layers palette
* [159715216](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/159715216) Maps: User can control connecting lines, appearance of points, and appearance of grid for each point layer individually
* [160190459](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/160190459) Maps: Hiding and showing cases works with multiple map layers
* [160593356](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/160593356) Graphs: Dragging an attribute from one axis to the other switches the placement of that attribute.
* [160693829](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/160693829) Graphs: When an attribute is dragged to a graph axis, the graph component becomes selected.

* [152220984](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152220984) Add Range button is in the Collector page but doesn't actually collect data
* [152287903](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152287903) TP-Sampler: with/without replacement options should be grayed out when model hidden
* [152288132](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152288132) TP-Sampler: Adding balls should continue number if present, or continue letters
* [152288179](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152288179) TP-Sampler: series dialog should show parse error

# Build 0446

CODAP Build 0446 was completed on Monday, June 4, 2018.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0446.
Notably, it includes a new feature: Data Cards.
Data Cards present an alternate tabular view of a data set.
Case Tables have a new icon on the left side of the title bar that toggle the
Case Table view to a Data Card View.

The release includes the following additions or changes to
the codeline since Build 0444.

features...
* [135869191](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/135869191) Users can reorder attribute in the attribute palette within a collection.
* [135869249](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/135869249) Users can drag attributes in an attribute palette from one collection to another and also to create a new top level collection.
* [150861351](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/150861351) Need API so plugins can signal change of persistent state
* [155689693](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/155689693) Case card shows info about attributes that varies according to the selection.
* [155689707](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/155689707) Show the name of the data context in the case card title bar
* [156019328](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/156019328) Icon to toggle between case card and case table has tooltip.
* [156060444](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/156060444) Case card has arrows that allow navigation from one case to the next.
* [157138864](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157138864) When there are only two categorical values, list them instead of saying \"2 values\"
* [157138949](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157138949) In the case card, indicate that an attribute has a formula display values with a gray text
* [157178986](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157178986) When switching to case table, scroll to the selected value(s)
* [157210469](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157210469) You can create a new attribute in the case card at any level
* [157224118](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157224118) setaside: improve performance further
* [157396720](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157396720) When >1 case at a child level is selected, parent levels reflect the range of values of the parents of those children.
* [157823640](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157823640) Reword collection header and make it become two lines when cases are set aside and table is too narrow to show full text
* [157849340](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157849340) Graph LSRLs can be made to respond dynamically to selection

bugs...
* [146032079](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/146032079) Destroying large dataset takes too long
* [150952852](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/150952852) Component API: update/component dimensions doesn't work
* [150992596](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/150992596) Plugin should not be able to create an attribute that has no name
* [152219351](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/152219351) Check for Select 0 items in Sample plugin
* [153065657](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/153065657) Undo of delete cases does not always bring back all the deleted cases
* [155270169](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/155270169) Cases do not have case IDs returned when using the DI Test API plugin
* [156213161](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/156213161) Plugins are not opening in specified location

# Build 0444

CODAP Build 0444 was completed on Wednesday, May 22, 2018 and released
on May 24.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0444.
Notably, it includes the ability to set aside cases and restore them at some later point.
The controls for this feature can be found under the "eye" menu of the Case Table inspector panel.
It also includes the following additions or changes to
the codeline since Build 0441.

* [157780336](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/157780336) Restore of set asides with parent collection is inconsistent

* [153759283](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/153759283) Removing legend and y-axis causes Reload dialog to come up in Mammals doc.
* [153962177](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/153962177) Onboarding plugin doesn't completely cover 2nd attribute task possibilities.
* [153983245](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/153983245) Case table crashes if you delete the only attribute.

* [154409616](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/154409616) ID for Draw Tool Plugin menu item has a space.
* [154647072](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/154647072) In embedded mode (e.g. ZiSci) marquee select in map is offset by the page scroll..

* [154682059](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/154682059) TypeError when bar graph is added to a Cat v Cat.

# Build 0377

CODAP build 0377 was completed on Tuesday, March 2, 2017 and released
on March 7.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0377.
Notably, this interval includes many additions to the Sampler plugin, the 
development cycle for which is not directly tied to the CODAP release cycle.
It includes the following additions or changes to
the codeline since Build 0375.

features...
* Sampler Features
  * [138756229](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138756229) Results are streamed to CODAP from the Mixer in the Sampler plug-in.
  * [138756611](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138756611) The user can Run, pause, and stop the simulation for the Sampler. 
  * [138756889](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138756889) Save state for Sampler plugin
  * [138757367](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138757367) The user can replace the default mixer with a spinner modeled on the TinkerPlots Sampler
  * [138859323](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138859323) A user can add and subtract balls in the Mixer in the Sampler plug-in.
  * [138859933](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138859933) A user can edit ball labels in the Mixer in the Sampler plug-in
  * [138859985](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138859985) The user sees a convincingly randomized animation of the balls in the Mixer of the Sampler plug-in.
  * [138860077](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138860077) The user can specify how many balls to draw in the Mixer in the Sampler plug-in.
  * [138860135](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/138860135) The user can specify the number of times to repeat mixing in the Mixer in the Sampler plug-in.

  * [139838905](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139838905) The user can add and delete wedges from the spinner.
  * [139839019](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139839019) The user can edit labels in the spinner.
  * [139839275](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139839275) The user can adjust the speed of the Sampler simulation.
  * [140188809](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140188809) The labels shown in the collection area take advantage of whatever space there is
  * [140187729](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140187729) Spinner is grayscale, not color
  * [140190187](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140190187) When sampling begins the case table is forced to appear
  * [140345487](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140345487) Indication on hover over balls that editing is possible
  * [140520605](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/140520605) Mixer balls are all blue
* [139984977](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139984977) In component mode, graph shows undo/redo icons
* [139985395](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139985395) In embedded mode components can be made to _always_ show their menu palette; i.e. without the user having to click on them.
* [139985563](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/139985563) In embedded mode, leaving the page happens without the warning dialog box that asks whether it's OK to lose your changes.

# Build 0357

CODAP build 0357 was completed Thursday, October 6, 2016.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0357.
The main work included in this build supports Version 2 of the Document Server API.
It includes the following additions or changes to
the codeline since Build 0354:

features...
* [128108423](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128108423) (DocStore V2) Make new CFM provider for version 2 document store API.
* [128108595](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128108595) (DocStore V2) A version 2 document store API that's given an accessKey in the URL can open that document.
* [128109015](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128109015) (DocStore V2) A version 2 document store API can save a document with its readWriteAccessKey.
* [128109077](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128109077) (DocStore V2) A version 2 document store API can patch a document with its readWriteAccessKey
* [128168463](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128168463) (DocStore V2) The version 2 document store API has an action for copying a shared document and returning the new copy's readWriteAccessKey and readOnlyAccessKey.
* [128435143](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128435143) (DocStore V2) Store a log of document access times.
* [128441585](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128441585) (DocStore V2) Create lara interactive page for the version 2 api.
* [128444429](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128444429) (DocStore V2) The version 2 document API should support the creation of shared copy documents.
* [128580507](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128580507) (DocStore V2) Add API to LARA which provides read/write access to a interactive run state. The actual story for this is in the LARA Portal project.
* [129633917](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/129633917) (DocStore V2) The document store should support copying a non-shared document given a source-id and and source-accesskey
* [129634099](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/129634099) (DocStore V2) The CFM should support a launchFromLara param that includes both a source-id and a source-accesskey
* [129634223](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/129634223) (DocStore V2) lara launch page should handle linked interactive state
* [130319855](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/130319855) Make collections headers somewhat distinct from attribute headers.
* [130320041](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/130320041) Make it easier to differentiate between attribute names that share text.
* [130376427](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/130376427) Text area should be selected after creation of a text box.
* [130777233](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/130777233) When there is no empty space, new components are added in the middle of the screen.
* [130920767](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/130920767) Date time axis should display data in chronological order

bugs...

* [116259539](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/116259539) Table lines not drawn correctly

* [123899721](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/123899721) Ability to prevent data set reorganization
* [128108157](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128108157) Improve and merge in work to support 2 document servers in CFM

* [128991945](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/128991945) Users can open documents of all readable mime types in CODAP (the default mime type is JSON).
* [129377111](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/129377111) CODAP documents can be created from Google Drive and placed in the correct folder.

* [116258177](http://www.pivotaltracker .com/projects/1055240/stories/116258177) Shift select in case table acts more like a command select.
* [116342103](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/116342103) Old files without extension not listed in Open dialog box?
* [120377399](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/120377399) Google save always saves at top level

* [127512431](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/127512431) File extension should be honored when saving to file system or Google Drive

* [129907199](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/129907199) DI Notification of Selection broken

# Build 0340

([#115345953](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/115345953)). The

unaffected. In addition, it includes the following additions or changes to

# Build 0330

CODAP build 0330 was completed Tuesday, March 8, 2016.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0330.
It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0329:

features:
* Support for qualitative data
  * [111171580](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/111171580) Allow an attribute to be declared with type 'qualitative' so that it displays to be qualitative rather than numeric.
  * [111171772](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/111171772) Qualitative graphs—iteration 0: In qualitative mode display numeric axes with no numbers, only 'low' corresponding to a value of zero and 'high' corresponding to a value of 100.
  * [111172714](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/111172714) Qualitative case table—iteration 1: Display numeric values as bars.
* Support for stand-alone mode
  * [112073351](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112073351) A URL parameter, standalone=true, tells CODAP not to display the tool shelf
  * [112073765](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112073765) In standalone mode, the data interactive should fill the document window and resize with the browser window. CODAP components should float on top of the DI and the space-finding algorithm should not regard the DI as taking up space.
  * [112074333](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112074333) A data interactive should be able to specify attributes by name and axis that get plotted on a graph when using the createComponent API.
  * [112074575](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112074575) In standalone mode a data interactive, when requesting that a component should be created, should be able to specify the 'position' as 'bottom' (default is 'top'), and a 'size'. If position is 'bottom' then the newly created component will be positioned as close to the bottom as possible.
  * [112074877](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112074877) In standalone mode, the data interactive should be allowed to make as many components of a given type as desired, unlike in normal mode where the interactive can only make one component of a given type.
  * [112077117](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112077117) Component inspectors should pin themselves to the right side of components instead of the right edge of the browser window.
* [113242533](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113242533) Users can drag attributes not just from tables but from wherever their names appear; e.g. graph axes.
* [113310447](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113310447) No reordering or deleting of case attributes if data interactive connected to context
* [113690729](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113690729) A graph with no attributes has text that tells the user to drop attributes
* [113690749](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113690749) On a graph with no attributes, user can click on the nascent axes to get a menu of attributes to apply to that axis.

bugs:
* [107877760](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/107877760) With CODAP started from https URL, the help page does not show.
* [109561442](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/109561442) Collapsing cases in a table that was generated by a data interactive does not toggle the collapse/expand icon
* [112545867](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112545867) Deleting attributes that creates empty table groups still has the table group separator.
* [112701823](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112701823) Deleting latitude or longitude attribute while a map is showing causes error in map.
* [112858973](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/112858973) Cannot dismiss tool palette
* [113010023](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113010023) Legend icon stays highlighted after deselecting points on graph.
* [113031089](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113031089) Delete an attribute while it is in a graph causes crash
* [113113519](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113113519) In case table, in presence of horizontal scrollbar, the last row cannot be made visible. Covered by scrollbar.
* [113642975](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113642975) Case table selection is not correct when cases are collapsed then expanded.
* [113883431](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/113883431) Deleting all cases in the case table keeps the expand collapse lines in the table group separator
* [114234465](http://www.pivotaltracker.com/projects/1055240/stories/114234465) Dot chart does not respond to changes in slider value

# Build 0321

CODAP build 0321 was completed Thursday, November 26, 2015.
It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0321. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0316:

Features:
* This build includes initial support for flexible grouping. Case tables can be reorganized by the user. A user can drag a column header to a new position
  in the same case table, from one table to another, or to the left to create a new case table in the same hierarchy. [97052848], [107435904], [98748712], [107434014]
* With this build, the undo and redo feature is substantially complete.
  * [107315094] Undo minimize tile
  * [107316766] Change in transparency of plot within a graph should be undoable
  * [107315618] Change in point size using the graph or map inspectors should be undoable
  * [107315952] Change in point color should be undoable
  * [107317100] Change in categorical legend category color should be undoable
  * [97261870] animate undo/redo of manual axis scaling
  * [107315274] Editing title of tile should be undoable
  * [104009308] Make the UndoHistory the locus for **all** logging of undoable user events.
  * [107433676] Undo/Redo of attribute drag
* Map improvements
  * [96682136] Map command for deleting selected cases
  * [108339046] On maps, do not show data tips with latitude and longitude. Only show data tips when there is a legend.
  * [108339212] For map grid keep the aspect ratio of rectangles ~1:1
  * [108339874] Data tip for map grid rectangle gives the number/percentage of each category represented
  * [96681400] In map, the Hide Points command should not hide connecting lines.
  * [108339628] Allow Click, Shift-click, and marquee selection with the map grid
* Saving and Sharing improvements
  * [105699908] When user makes copy of the document prevent them from using a name that is the same as an existing name.
  * [105699432] Store documents opened by shared links in memory and require save to have them saved to the user's document store.
  * [105698040] When saving a file the user is told if an existing document with that name already exists and allows them to choose another name or overwrite the existing file.
* [104946244] Need to be able to change order of Axis Labels in Graph
* [105050722] Can point outline default color be gray instead of white?

Bugs:

# Build 0316

CODAP build 0316 was completed Friday, October 23, 2015. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0316. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0313:

Features:
* Undo/Redo is enabled by default. Most user significant actions can be undone and redone. The undo stack has no upper limit, but can be cleared if
  a user performs a significant action for which undo/redo has not yet been
  implemented. This constraint is in place to avoid corruption of the document.
* [104674906] If data interactive has no title, user has no way of knowing they can name the tile

Bugs:
* [#105699908] When user makes copy of the document prevent them from using a name that is the same as an existing name.
* [104648952] Editable tile title conflicts with dragging tile. Should be able to click and drag in title without triggering editing mode.
* [105698400] The document ID should always match the document content.
* [105865536] Selected cases in an exported file are not selected when file is re-opened
* [104671544] Can't click on axis label to change it
* [105812718] Can't double-click slider name to edit
* [104590756] Graph with 2 attributes on y-axis plus an attribute on 2nd y-axis does not restore properly.
* [105684150] Wrongly colored key to attribute color with multiple attributes on y-axis
* [105266378] Can't change graph legend when dropping attribute in legend area.
* [104664476] Can't click on points that are close to the right edge of the graph
* [105152272] Icons problems
* [104669060] Hover on points are not the same color if user changes point colors
* [105247632] Remove snapshot option from inspector for map (until we get it implemented)
* [105867104] Axis point color does not match the color of the associated points on the graph
* [105882210] Display Web page is limited to concord.org sites only
* [105608976] Tile list menu should use moonicon font, not svg's.
* [105886546] Moves with new components do not make it to the undo redo stack
* [106337060] Minimized tiles don't unminimize after export or save

# Build 0300

CODAP build 0300 was completed Friday, July 10, 2015. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0300. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0298:

This release includes a partial implementation of Undo/Redo. To avoid confusion,
the feature is disabled by default. It can be enabled by appending the
query parameter, "Undo=true", to the CODAP URL. The undo/redo feature keeps
an undo history of unlimited depth, so a user can theoretically undo actions
back to the beginning of their current CODAP session. In the current
implementation, however, any action that does not yet implement undo will cause
the undo history to be cleared. This is to prevent corruption of the document.
Here are stories related to undo:

* [96193934] Buttons in the tools shelf labeled \"Undo\" and \"Redo\" enable and disable according to whether there is an action available.
* [96194856] Undo/redo of a single move or resize of a component (tile) frame is possible.
* [96195672] Undo/redo of dragging an attribute to a graph axis
* [96196194] Undo history can be longer than one command.
* [96678768] Undo/Redo—Create graph, map, slider, text
* [96679174] Undo/redo 'Tables'
* [96679234] Undo/redo 'Calc'
* [96680608] Undo/redo of graph gear menu commands
* [96680770] Undo/redo of Delete Selected Cases command
* [96698306] An error should be raised if an unexpected command is received by Undo/Redo.
* [96701234] Undo/redo close component (data interactive)
* [97330262] Problems with slider undo
* [97839646] Undo/Redo should be capable of being disabled

Features:
* [96096506] Data tip should not come up while marquee selecting in a graph
* [96097214] CODAP should have a way to \"screen capture\" a component tile that can be exported as png or JPEG
* [98116266] With an empty graph, dropping an attribute in the plot area should be equivalent to dropping on the x-axis.
* [98161538] Provide function greatCircleDistance

Bug Fixes:
* [84069882] Row number for data in CODAP now read NaN.
* [96155866] Narrow categorical x-axis cases column and count numbers do not align with data points
* [96157690] If URL has a run key or record id, cannot open a document after closing current document.
* [96941770] Graphs saved as images should show selection
* [97054304] Scale graph to data animate-option is buggy
* [97250778] Saves fail if document includes a web view.
* [97536050] Collection's caseIDToIndexMap seems broken
* [97587192] Using the slider to change the size of the grid in a map causes a crash(299)
* [97669190] Default case name of 'children' is confusing
* [97735800] Title bar in guide window does not show the title when document is first loaded.
* [97861140] CSVs with numeric headers are not handled correctly.
* [97935474] Drop CSV file waits for mouse move to show table.
* [98023842] Categorical attributes do not work on the second vertical axis, and when they are removed it throws an exception
* [98031548] Rescale does not work for a plotted function
* [98053562] If parent case is deleted, Data Interactive may throw exception when new cases are created

# Build 0292

CODAP build 0292 was completed Thursday, April 9. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0292. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0286:

* *Feature* Second vertical axis. For numeric graphs you can drag an attribute
  to create a new axis on the right side, the range of which is independent of the
  lefthand axis.
* *Feature* 'User Entry' dialog. At start-up or document close we present a dialog that presents all the ways a user can find a new document to work with,
  including example documents, file system documents, and Concord cloud documents.
* *Feature* Add a wait cursor during document load.[PT 83859012]
* *Infrastructure* Reorganize document related functionality to be more modular
  and support storage plugins.
* *Data Interactive API* Add deleteCases action. Supports Ocean Tracks Track Selector Tool.
* *Bug Fixes* [PT 89563146, 89464236, 89715094, 89563146, 89714850, 85718994, 88171616, 89224096, 89563146, 89568576, 89570550, 89576510, 89966622, 90154940, 90201376, 90315114, 90530596, 90595014, 90595172, 90733844, 90786658, 91229718, 91286396, 91612558 ]

# Build 0286

CODAP build 0286 was completed Monday, March 2. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0286. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0280:

* *Feature* Multiple Data Contexts and Data Interactives: CODAP had been
  restricted to a single one- or two-level data hierarchy and to a single Data
  Interactive providing this data. These restrictions have been removed. Now, an instance of CODAP can manage multiple Data Contexts (data sets) and multiple
  Data Interactives simultaneously. This work was done to support the Building
  Models project. This change incorporates a number of UI changes, including:

  * The "Case Table" button has been changed to "Case Tables" and will display
    one Case Table for each existing Data Context.
  * Case Tables now has a heading with the name of its data context.
  * The "Clear All Data" button has been removed.
  * A Case Table gear menu item, "select all",  has been added.
  * Graphs will only display their default axes if there is only one Data
    Context.
  * Each graph presents data from a single Data Context, so if you drag an attribute from a different Case Table than the attributes on other
    axes, all existing attribute associations will be removed.
* *Feature* Graphs initialize with an "explosion of dots" animation.
* *Feature* Better error handling for errors on file load or import [PT 86659222](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/86659222)
* *Feature* Splash screen [PT 87225000](https://www.pivotaltracker
.com/story/show/87225000)
* *Feature* Busy cursor during document load.[PT 83859012](https://www.pivotaltracker
.com/story/show/83859012)
* *Feature* URL syntax for Data Interactives simplified. Formerly:
`?moreGames=[{name=..., url=...}]`. Now: `?di-...`.
* *Integration* The "help" menu item is now directed to the new CODAP help site.
* *Integration* CODAP now logs user messages to the Log Manager.
* *Integration* Tracking of production CODAP enabled for Google Analytics.
* *Bug Fix* Attributes marked as categorical in initGame are plotted as such.
  [PT 88927776](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88927776)
* *Bug Fixes* Numerous improvements to document operations involving the
  Document Server, including
  * [PT 87584868](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/87584868) CODAP crash attempting to autosave after session time out
  * [PT 88182120](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88182120),
  * [PT 88253392](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88253392) Firefox doesn't support synchronous sub-document loading
  * [PT 88750194](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88750194) Untitled document in URL causes error message to pop up
  * [PT 87726326](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/87726326)
  * [PT ](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/) Data fork and resource fork files should be deleted at the same time in the document server
  * [PT 87583594](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/87583594) Revert to original of an imported document reverts to the first auto-saved state
  * [PT 88253392](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88253392)
  * [PT 88660014](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88660014) Ensure the document server url has a trailing slash
  * [PT 85027774](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85027774) Proactively update the url shown in the browser nav bar
  * [PT  87580028](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/87580028) Export through the file menu should export everything within one file
  * [PT 86637300](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/86637300) Only call rename on the document server if we have an external document id
* [PT 88960384](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/88960384) Parent and child case counts do not come up in the table title bar if no change has happened to the data.
* *Bug Fix* Workaround for Safari refusal to download export [PT 77733392](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77733392)

# Build 0280

Build 0280 was completed Tuesday, January 27. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0280. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0279:

* *Feature* Native CODAP form for users to provide feedback and report bugs.
* *Bug Fix* Graph, Map, and Table buttons were active during document load. Deactivate to prevent broken components. [PT 83354032](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/83354032)
* *Bug Fix* Data tips for map grid use collection-specified labels for showing the count [PT 85743504](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85743504)
* *Bug Fix* Make Guide URLs schemes case insensitive. [PT 85620698](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85620698)

# Build 0279

Build 0279 was completed Wednesday, January 7. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0279. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0277:

* *Feature* Various enhancements to improve handling of autosave.
  * Transfers to and from the document server are compressed.
    [PT 85099042](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85099042)
  * Data portion of document is handled separately so changes to data do not
    require autosave of main document and changes not involving data do not
    require autosave of the data portion.
    [PT 84488450](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84488450)
  * The data portion can save incrementally. The unchanging portion no longer is transferred every time.
    [PT 84489824](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84489824)
* *Feature* Add the ability to drag the URL for a Data Interactive or web page
  to the workspace. [PT 84647078](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84647078)
* *Feature* When not in Document Server environment, default to guest
  authorization and omit login page.[PT 85035120](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85035120)
* *Bug Fix* Global Values could break document save.
[PT 85331400](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/85331400)

# Build 0277

Build 0277 was completed Friday, December 19. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0277. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0276:

* *Bug Fix* It is now possible to specify http or https links in the Guide
  authoring dialog. [PT 84773846](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84773846)

# Build 0276

Build 0276 was completed Monday, December 15. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0276. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0274:

* *Feature* Drag and drop to import CODAP Documents: Drag a document from the
  file system to the CODAP work area to open the document. [PT 74242252](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74242252)
* *Feature* Drag and drop to import CSV Documents: Drag a CSV file (comma
  separated values) or a tab delimited file from the file system to the CODAP work area to open as a new CODAP document. [PT 84590044](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84590044)
* *Usability* Reorder items in case table gear menu to be more natural. Add
  column to parent table now comes before add column to child table.
* *Usability* Category labels align with bottom of univariate stacks.
[PT 79906178](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/79906178)
* *Bug Fixes* 'prev', 'next', 'first', and 'last' formula functions, and the
'caseIndex' formula value now produce correct results. [PT 84052752](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84052752), [PT 84108898](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84108898), [PT 84146702](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84146702), and [PT 84069882](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84069882).
* *Bug Fix* Close document after rename now works. [PT 84134420](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/84134420)
* *Bug Fix* Suppress inappropriate file menu items for documents not yet saved.
[PT 81150058](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/81150058)
* *Bug Fix* Delay autosave to prevent race conditions on slow networks. [PT 83217612](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/83217612)

# Build 0274

Build 0274 was completed Thursday, December 4. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0274. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0271:

* *Performance* Reformed and simplified how CODAP manages case data collections.
[PT 80746938](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80746938)
* *Usability* Synchronize page title with document name. Previously page title
  was associated with the Data Interactive and did not necessarily update
  properly. [PT 79811988](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/79811988)
* *Usability* Options Menu updated [PT 74730434]((https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74730434)
* *Bug Fix* Remove reference to games from "clear data" button. [PT 77890436](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77890436)
* *Bug Fix* Fix crash on unsuccessful rename. [PT 81497420](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/81497420)
* *Bug Fix* Map component changes not triggering autosave. [PT 80417690](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80417690)
* *Bug Fix* Hiding all cases in a map caused a crash [PT 82219920](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/82219920)
* *Bug Fix* Show connecting lines caused a crash [PT 83343306](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/83343306)
* *Bug Fix* Improved Importer handling of malformed or unexpected CSV format.

# Build 0271

Build 0271 was completed Wednesday, November 5. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0271. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0270:

* *Feature*: Map Grids. Defines a grid overlay on a map. The grid cells are
  colored proportionally to the population of cases within. The size of grid cells
  is adjustable from a slider control. When a grid is present, the dots
  representing cases can be hidden or displayed.

* Favicon has been updated to the new CODAP logo.

# Build 0270

Build 0270 was completed Tuesday, October 21. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0270. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0269:

* Revert [PT 80227482](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80227482). It
  was found that this optimization caused saved documents to omit some components.
* *Usability*: Run number toggles in graph displays now behave as true toggles.
[PT 80848944](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80848944)
* *Performance*: Implemented a couple of optimizations to existing data model:
(a) avoid cloning cases when creating model, and (b) remove is-sorted test from
  DG.query. [PT 80867286](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80867286)

# Build 0269

Build 0269 was completed Wednesday, October 9. It can be found here:
http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0269. It includes the following
additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0265:

* *Pull Request*: Merge work from wip-autosave. This is a batch of changes
  including support for auto-save and improved integration between CODAP, and
  Concord Consortium's LARA, and the Document Manager. User controls around
  document management were adjusted accordingly. When autosave is active, the
  normal save button is suppressed. A title bar was added above the toolshelf. See
[Pivotal Tracker Epic](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/epic/show/1378916) for
  details.
* *Pull Request*: Save and restore cross-domain interactives via postMessage.
  Add support for save and restore of Data Interactive state when using the
  PostMessage Data Interactive API.

* *Usability*: [PT #80161868](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80161868)
In DG.mainPage.addGraph removed check for &#39;gameIsReady&#39; as an obstacle
  to being able to creating a graph in these documents.
* *Formulas*: [PT #80229148](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80229148)
Added *secondsToDate* and *month* to the list of functions available in the
  formula editor. Implemented these functions in FormulaContext.
* *Performance*: [PT #80227482](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80227482)
Removed call to DG.store.commitRecords in DG.Component.createComponent because
  it caused (apparently unnecessary) performance degradation in creation of
  components.
* *Bug Fix*: [PT #80161868](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/80161868)
Connect documents to their proper contexts when opening.
* *Bug Fixes*: Fix several bugs where CODAP state changes did not mark the current document as 'dirty' and in need of save. These bugs were exposed by
  the auto-save changes.
* *Bug Fix*: "sum function is fooled by numbers masquerading as strings" [PT 76152402](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/76152402)

# Build 0265

Build 0265 was completed Wednesday, September 10. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0265. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0261:

* *Data Interactives API*: Add ability to reset data collections [PT 74726740](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74726740)
* *Usability*: Delay appearance of data tips over map areas [PT 75753028](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/75753028)
* *Usability*: Display counts of parent and child collections in case table [74002794](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74002794)
* *Bug Fix*: "Latest" release link was broken. Fixed. [PT 78067876](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/78067876)
* *Bug Fix*: Login dialog should provide feedback for unsuccessful login [PT 77822642](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77822642)

# Build 0261

Build 0261 was completed Wednesday, August 27. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0261. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0259:

* *Usability*: Enlarged the initial map size. [PT 77589472](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77589472)
* *Usability*: Support multiple selectable map backgrounds [PT 77589672](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77589672)
* *Usability*: Implement save and restore of map state [PT 74789326](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74789326)
* *Bug fix*: Fixed bug where map zoom controls were always on top, even with respect to other components. [PT 72746948](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/72746948)
* *Bug fix*: Column names 'LAT' and 'LONG' were not recognized as indicating coordinate types. [PT 77589342](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77589342)

# Build 0259

Build 0259 was completed Wednesday August 20. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0259. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0258:

* *Usability*: Improved tool tips: they now disappear when mouse is out of area.[PT 74788562](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74788562)
* *Bug fix*: Maps: When you'd added an attribute you couldn't remove it. [PT 74886372](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74886372)
* *Bug fix*: When a new map is displayed the area of interest would not fill the screen space, as intended. [PT 74888286](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74888286).
* *Bug fix*: Exception thrown by Data Interactive during document save was not being caught, causing the document save to fail. Now it is caught and logged. [PT 75364516](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/75364516)
* *Bug fix*: Access to Mongo document store was broken. This was a regression introduced by a merge error when introducing the document store feature. [PT 76807090](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/76807090)
* *Bug fix*: Redirection was broken for relocatable CODAP. Replaced html redirection with javascript redirection. [PT 77210916](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/77210916)

# Build 0258

Build 0258 was completed Wednesday August 13. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0258. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since Build 0256:

* Integration of changes that permit CODAP users to save and restore CODAP documents to the new document server.

* Integration of improvements to the CODAP Data Interactives API to permit Data Interactives to communicate with their containing CODAP instance via PostMessage. This overcomes same origin issues, and makes it easier for CODAP to run Data Interactives from affiliated partners without special server configuration.

* Fixed regression caused by RaphaelExt:removeClass fix. PlotLayer was using broken removeClass improperly.

* Permit area map to display boundaries in parent collection. [PT #76153432](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/76153432)

# Build 0256

Build 0256 was completed Wednesday July 30. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0256. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since build_0254:

* Fix to Raphael Extensions library, raphaelExt.js. The methods, addClass and removeClass handled string operations improperly. [PT 74247542](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74247542)

* Make activity log server URL configurable. [PT 74726882](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74726882)

* Match log message syntax to that expected by Data Analytics Log Server. [PT 74726922](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74726922)

* Fix bug in which data tips no longer showed on maps. [PT 74983698](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74983698)

* Fix bug where categorical attributes were displayed as if numeric in map legends.[PT 75815320](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/75815320)

# Build 254

Build 0254 was completed Wednesday July 23. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0254. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since build_0253:

* Page title is no longer "Data Games". Now it is "CODAP - " and the name of the current activity. [PT #74730648](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74730648)

* Fix bug affecting maps with area bounds which causes the map to do an extreme zoom in unintentionally.[PT #74886150](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74886150)

* Fix bug causing crash when executing "delete selected cases" on an empty case table. [PT #72571004](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/72571004)

# Build 253

Build 0253 was completed Wednesday July 16. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0253. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since build_0252:

* Remove references to Data Games. Replace with "CODAP". [PT #74730314](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74730314) and [PT #74730648] (https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74730648)
* Fix bug in graphs with Legends, cases with no value should be colored gray. [PT #74260978](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74260978)
* Enable runtime configuration in general, and configuration of what buttons and menus appear on the tool-shelf and in what order in particular. [PT #69466388](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/69466388) and [PT #73645766](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/73645766)
* Fix bug affecting dot plots and dot charts when space is small. [PT #73005260](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/73005260)
* Display map regions colored by aggregate attribute. [PT #74314524](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/74314524)

# Build 252

Build 0252 was completed Tuesday July 8. It can be found here: http://codap.concord.org/releases/build_0252. It includes the following additions or changes to the codeline since build_0248:

* Dependency on Protovis library removed. Fixes: [PT #58197034](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/58197034)
* Enable import of CODAP document from file system. This replaces import from a selection string. Completes [PT #73842232](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/73842232)
* Enable export of CODAP document as a file. This replaces export to a selection string. Completes [PT #73843462](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/73843462)
* Always expose developer menu items in the "File" menu. Previously these were suppressed in the production web sites. This is a transitional change until work on the document server is completed and rules for the file open and file save menu items are settled.
* Added windows build instructions to github readme. Fixes [PT# 70523472](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/70523472).
  Uniformly handle empty values in simple popups. We now disable the submit button. Before various dialogs handled the situation in a variety of ways.
* Remove misleading hint from unvariate graph "plot value" feature. Fixes [PT #70093080](https://www.pivotaltracker.com/story/show/70093080) and [github #3](https://github.com/concord-consortium/codap/issues/3).
